#2181. La moviola dell'ultimo turno di A: dal mani di Joao Mario al mancato rosso a Marcandalli

Non sono mancati gli episodi da moviola nell'ultimo turno di campionato:

In Parma-Bologna Luca Zufferli ha bisogno del VAR per correggere parecchie - troppe - decisioni, ma è anche vero che la prima chiamata indirizza la gara. Il VAR porta all’espulsione di Ndiaye, e di tutte è quella che convince meno. Il difensore trattiene Odgaard, che a sua volta gli afferra i pantaloncini: il corpo a corpo va valutato nella sua interezza, senza isolare soltanto il gesto del giocatore del Parma. È molto difficile considerare la valutazione da campo un chiaro ed evidente errore: il VAR, tante volte spento in questa stagione, non avrebbe dovuto intervenire. Da lì in poi è un susseguirsi di chiamate da Lissone: prima viene corretto il rigore indicato per un contatto fuori area tra Keita e Bernardeschi, poi servono altre due revisioni per assegnare i penalty su Cambiaghi e Fabbian. Netto il primo, per l’intervento in ritardo di Touré; più discutibile il secondo, insufficiente la prestazione.

In Genoa-Milan, la scelta di Davide Massa di ammonire soltanto Marcandalli scontenta i rossoneri e convince poco. Rabiot viene fermato mentre può attaccare verso la porta, e Cisse è a due passi: la distanza è molto ampia e questo lascia qualche dubbio sul DOGSO, ma distanza è un concetto molto relativo. Conta lo sviluppo possibile dell’azione, insieme alla possibilità concreta di recupero degli altri difensori: il rosso, tutto sommato, ci sarebbe sembrato più giusto del giallo, che non è uno scandalo. Corretto il mancato intervento del VAR, come pure l’annullamento per fuorigioco del gol di Osmajic, in una partita segnata anche dalle proteste e dalle espulsioni nelle panchine.

Il francese si è poi sfogato sui social, indirizzando un messaggio all'arbitro: "Nella vita come nel calcio si possono fare errori, bisogna almeno saperli ammettere. Mentire continuamente non vi farà andare avanti. Ed è il calcio italiano a subirne le conseguenze.. Pensateci. Bonne soirée".

In merito si è espresso l'ex arbitro Giampaolo Calvarese: "I quattro parametri da rispettare sono: la probabilità di mantenere o guadagnare il controllo del pallone, il numero dei difendenti, la direzione generale dell'azione e la distanza dalla porta. Siamo a 60-65 metri dalla porta. Sicuramente un'occasione da gol, sicuramente se avesse dato rosso non sarebbe intervenuto il VAR, sicuramente una decisione difficile. A livello geografico, visto che siamo in questa posizione, ho detto per questo che fosse una scelta sostenibile".

Il tocco di braccio di Joao Mario durante Fiorentina-Juventus è stato oggetto di grandi dibattiti nel dopo partita. E di tante valutazioni arbitrali. L'ex designatore Gianluca Rocchi e l'apprezzato commentatore di Dazn Luca Marelli sono stati concordi nel bocciare la decisione dell'arbitro Colombo e del Var. Per entrambi quel "contatto" doveva essere punito con il calcio di rigore.

Ancora una volta finiscono sotto accusa il designatore Orsato e la gestione del Var. Un tormentone insopportabile che va avanti dall'inizio del torneo. Il nuovo corso arbitrale non convince per quanto riguarda l'uso del mezzo tecnologico. Le linee guida imposte dal nuovo allenatore degli arbitri sono continuamente oggetto di critiche. E così è successo anche al Franchi. Perché non richiamare Colombo davanti al video? Perché non dare al direttore di gara la possibilità di rivalutare la sua decisione sfruttando il Var. Un qualcosa che deve essere considerato un "alleato", non un nemico con il quale doversi confrontare.

#2180. John Beaton sparks Celtic TV panic over Yang 'red card' as ref confession aired

Celtic TV commentators were left fearing Yang was for the off after John Beaton appeared to reach for his pocket against Kilmarnock.

The South Korean, already on a booking, challenged Nicky Cleșcenco and sparked panic in the commentary box when Beaton initially looked ready to produce a card.

But no foul was given and Yang escaped what could have been a costly second yellow.

The incident, also highlighted on the BBC's live feed, had Donnelly fearing the worst for the returning winger, who had been booked for a crude challenge earlier in the half.

Read more: The Celtic star ribbed by Martin O'Neill for 'trying to be Messi-like'

Speaking on Celtic TV, Donnelly said: "I thought John Beaton was going to his pocket. I don't think it was a foul, though. I think the lad just fell.

"It's a weird one. John Beaton went to go to his pocket, no sooner had he done it and he changed his mind."

Yang was ultimately able to breathe a sigh of relief as Beaton waved play on and kept his cards in his pocket.

#2179. Four chances created: West Brom starlet impresses in long-awaited league debut for loan club after challenging spell

West Brom signed two new right-backs in Nolan Galves and Brayann Pereira in the summer transfer window. It meant first-team opportunities were set to be limited for WBA academy prospect Alex Williams, so a loan move to Scottish Premiership side Hibernian was sanctioned.

Frustratingly, Williams’ stint at Easter Road has been challenging. The manager who signed him, David Gray, didn’t give the Baggies prospect many opportunities and he was then sacked.

Williams had played just 11 minutes before Saturday’s trip to Pittodrie to face Aberdeen. However, the 21-year-old was called into action when Felix Passlack was forced off early on, and he took his chance to impress in a rare opportunity.

Passlack has been the favoured starter on the right for Hibernian, including under new boss Marinek Reedijk. Kanayo Megwa was his backup early in the campaign before injury hit but West Brom’s Williams was still finding opportunities hard to come by.

A chance came his way on Saturday though, and he took it. Williams played 81 minutes as Hibs ran out 4-0 winners against the Dons. He provided a complete performance at right-back, offering attacking threat and defensive solidity.

The WBA talent created four chances and completed 30 of his 33 passes for a completion rate of 91%. He was frequently trusted in possession with 56 touches and he saw to his defensive duties impressively too, winning four of his seven ground duels while also completing two tackles, three clearances and two interceptions (stats via FotMob).

Williams’ performance was a testament to his attitude. Despite incredibly limited chances to date, he was sharp and ready to step up for Hibs when they needed him, and that can not go unnoticed.

West Brom and Williams will be hoping this serves as a turning point in his Hibernian loan. The extent of Passlack’s injury remains to be seen, but it could give the Baggies loanee a chance to make the starting spot his own.

With more performances like the one against Aberdeen, Williams can ease the blow of losing Passlack. It will also strengthen his chances of keeping a place in the plans of new boss Meerdijk.

WBA are sure to be monitoring his situation closely. If minutes remain hard to come by, the Championship side will not hesitate to bring Williams back and send him out on loan to a new club – which they can do, as his only appearance before joining Hibs came in the Carabao Cup.

Williams can find the minutes he needs and deserves in Passlack’s absence, so it remains to be seen just how long he faces out.

#2176. Aquilani saluta Sassuolo dopo 13 partite e un crollo inaspettato. Pronto D'Aversa

Il dado è tratto in casa Sassuolo, la società neroverde ha deciso di esonerare Alberto Aquilani e ora la notizia è anche ufficiale. Si legge, nel comunicato ufficiale degli emiliani: "L’U.S. Sassuolo Calcio comunica di aver sollevato l’allenatore Alberto Aquilani e il suo staff dai rispettivi incarichi. La Società ringrazia il tecnico e i suoi collaboratori per l’impegno profuso e la serietà dimostrata alla guida dei neroverdi e augura loro le migliori fortune per il futuro". Poche righe, a concludere un'avventura iniziato sotto tutti altri auspici.

42 anni, il tecnico chiude la sua esperienza in Emilia dopo 13 partite, nelle quali ha ottenuto 4 vittorie, 2 pareggi e 7 sconfitte. Paga il rendimento dell'ultimo mese e mezzo. Dopo la vittoria contro la Juventus datata 13 settembre, la squadra ha avuto un crollo verticale, ottenendo un solo punto nelle seguenti 7 partite: 1-1 contro il Milan. Cinque sconfitte consecutive e una difesa che ha palesato enormi problemi, basti pensare ai 6 gol incassati dalla Lazio o ai 4 da Fiorentina e Frosinone. Fatale il ko contro la Roma, ironia del destino la sua squadra del cuore nonché quella in cui è cresciuto calcisticamente.

Per la successione è pronto Roberto D'Aversa. Manca l'ufficialità ma ormai ci siamo: pronto un contratto fino al termine della stagione, al 30 giugno 2027, con opzione per andare avanti assieme un altro anno.

#2175. Birmingham City set to welcome back James Beadle for Stoke City clash, but doubts remain over six-goal star

Birmingham City have gone into the November international break top of the Championship. BCFC have been really impressive to start the year, and have continued winning games, even with some injuries to key players.

Chris Davies’ side have gone the their last two games without goalkeeper James Beadle and top-scorer August Priske. However, as reported by Birmingham Live, Davies has provided an update on the pairs fitness going into the internationals.

“James (Beadle) definitely will be (back),” Davies confirmed but he couldn’t be so sure on Priske. “August (Priske) did everything to get ready but he couldn’t,” the Blues manager said.

“I presume he is going to make more strides in the next week or so. I’d hope he was back after the international break but I couldn’t be definite on that because it’s how it settles down.”

Davies also provided an update on Marc Leonard who is not part of the Birmingham City squad after being omitted at the start of the season following an Achilles injury. The BCFC boss said he is looking to be back in action after the New Year.

Both Beadle and Priske could return for Birmingham City’s trip to Stoke City on 21 November, but the striker’s availability would be the bigger boost.

Beadle is the established first-choice goalkeeper after missing the wins over Millwall and Swansea with a knock. Davies has confirmed he will be available for Stoke, although Ryan Allsop has impressed in his absence.

Priske’s return is less certain, but potentially more significant. The forward provides their main focal point in attack. Davies is hopeful the international break will give him enough time to recover, but has stopped short of guaranteeing his availability.

While BCFC have continued scoring without him, having their main goal threat back for such an important away fixture would be a considerable boost.

Priske has been a key player for Birmingham City this season and has really come into his own after a slow start last term. However, his injury has not slowed up the Blues as many might’ve thought.

Davies has adjusted his side well and in Priske’s absence, BCFC have still been an attacking threat. Max Bird has come into the side as part of the front four with great success.

Jay Stansfield has also found his scoring touch with three goals in his last two games. The Blues have adjusted very well to Priske’s absence, and while they were prefer to have their top-scorer in action, the club have shown they are still strong enough without him.

#2174. Oscar Cortes transforms Rangers fortunes as stunning form revival earns Colombia call

Rangers misfit Oscar Cortes has sparked an incredible career U-turn after his eye-catching form earned him an unlikely Colombia call-up.

The wide man is rebuilding his career with Huracan in Argentina during a season-long loan that is due to expire in the coming weeks. Cortes has made 31 league appearances, scoring three goals and providing five assists during an impressive campaign.

Big things were expected of the 22-year-old when he arrived in Glasgow on an initial loan deal, which eventually turned into a £4million permanent transfer and he remains under contract at Ibrox until 2029, although Huracan retain an option to buy.

But the former Lens man struggled at Ibrox, with injuries and a lack of consistent form pushing him down the pecking order.

Read more: Premiership star bemoans 'unlucky' red card picked up against Rangers

He later moved to Sporting Gijon, but it is his return to South America that has reignited his career and earned him a fresh opportunity with his national team.

Cortes has been drafted into Colombia's squad as a replacement for the injured Jaminton Campaz.

He will now link up with Celtic forward Camilo Duran for a double-header against Chile in the coming days.

#2171. Calciomercato no stop - Indiscrezioni, trattative e retroscena del 10 novembre

Accostato alla Roma nelle scorse settimane, l'attaccante esterno del Club Brugge Carlos Forbs pare non essere uscito dai radar di D'Amico, ma ha tanti ammiratori in giro per l'Europa. Secondo le informazioni pubblicate dal Sun infatti, la società giallorossa lo segue insieme a Tottenham, Arsenal, Manchester United e Fiorentina. Non si tratta però di un'operazione da fare nel corso della sessione invernale di mercato, il club belga infatti lo cederà solamente la prossima estate, come da prassi per quanto riguarda il mercato in uscita del club belga.

Tra Noa Lang e la tifoseria del Napoli la frattura è ormai evidente. Contro la Lazio, al Maradona, l’esterno olandese ha cercato il sostegno della gente portandosi le mani alle orecchie, in un gesto simile a un "non vi sento". La risposta della curva, però, è stata all'insegna... dei fischi. I tifosi non gli hanno perdonato quanto accaduto a Porto: dopo la sconfitta per 4-1, Lang aveva lasciato infatti il campo senza salutare il settore ospiti. Da allora il rapporto tra queste due componenti del mondo partenopeo si è profondamente incrinato. Il Napoli ha assoluta necessità di intervenire sul mercato e questo a prescindere dai risultati che verranno conseguiti da qui a fine dicembre. Il centrocampo è il settore nel quale il direttore sportivo Manna è pronto a intervenire con decisione vista la situazione di incertezza legata al futuro di Anguissa e Lobotka. Coulibaly del Monaco è la primissima scelta, ma la trattativa come detto più volte non è certo semplice visto che per il classe 2004 il club del Principato chiede tantissimo. Sempre rimanendo al Monaco piace Camara, ma il discorso anche in questo caso è complicato. E allora i riflettori del Napoli sono puntati anche su altri giocatori. Una pista da non sottovalutare è quella che porta ad Adem Zorgane dell’Union Saint-Gilloise, che negli ultimi anni è diventata una società in grado di mettere in mostra tanti talenti.

Mario Hermoso è diventato uno dei simboli della Roma di Gasperini. Lo spagnolo classe 1995 interpreta il ruolo di difensore in maniera moderna e aggressiva, anticipando gli avversari e partecipando costantemente alla manovra offensiva. Da quando lavora con Gasp ha realizzato sette reti, tre delle quali in questa stagione. Numeri che testimoniano una trasformazione sorprendente per un giocatore cresciuto presso la scuola del Cholo Simeone. L’attuale accordo scadrà il prossimo 30 giugno, ma la volontà di proseguire insieme è reciproca. Le parti sarebbero vicine a un’intesa fino al 2029, con opzione per il 2030. Hermoso sarebbe inoltre disposto ad accettare una riduzione dell’ingaggio pur di continuare la propria avventura nella Capitale.

Il dado è tratto in casa Sassuolo, la società neroverde ha deciso di esonerare Alberto Aquilani e ora la notizia è anche ufficiale. Si legge, nel comunicato ufficiale degli emiliani: "L’U.S. Sassuolo Calcio comunica di aver sollevato l’allenatore Alberto Aquilani e il suo staff dai rispettivi incarichi. La Società ringrazia il tecnico e i suoi collaboratori per l’impegno profuso e la serietà dimostrata alla guida dei neroverdi e augura loro le migliori fortune per il futuro".

Il Sassuolo è pronto a stringere sul profilo di Roberto D'Aversa come suo prossimo allenatore. Vi avevamo dato conto della preferenza dei neroverdi che si stava direzionando verso il tecnico abruzzese e anche adesso raccogliamo conferme. Pronto per l'ex Torino, Empoli e Lecce tra le altre un contratto fino al termine della stagione, al 30 giugno 2027, con opzione per andare avanti assieme un altro anno.

Il Sassuolo ha il suo nuovo allenatore. Roberto D’Aversa firmerà il suo contratto con il club neroverde fino a giugno con opzione. In sostanza manca solo l’annuncio ufficiale visto che gli accordi verbali sono stati tutti raggiunti nel tardo pomeriggio. Insomma D’Aversa è pronto a cominciare questa sua nuova avventura dopo l’esperienza molto positiva nel finale della scorsa stagione alla guida del Torino. Il tecnico si metterà subito a lavoro nelle prossime ore cercando di sfruttare al meglio questa sosta, anche se diversi giocatori saranno impegnati con le rispettive Nazionali.

Il futuro di Julian Alvarez e Martin Zubimendi potrebbe incrociarsi. Secondo alcune indiscrezioni provenienti dalla Spagna, Arsenal e Atletico Madrid starebbero valutando una doppia operazione che coinvolgerebbe due giocatori arrivati all'estate con grandi aspettative ma protagonisti di momenti complicati. L'attaccante argentino aveva manifestato in estate la volontà di lasciare l'Atletico per trasferirsi al Barcellona, ma il club madrileno ha bloccato la sua partenza. L'inizio di stagione, però, non è stato quello sperato: appena due gol in 12 presenze per la squadra di Diego Simeone, dopo i 20 realizzati in 49 partite nella scorsa annata. Nonostante il momento difficile, il tecnico argentino continua a difenderlo. Dopo la sconfitta contro il Barcellona, Simeone ha ribadito la necessità di ritrovare "il miglior Julian", convinto che l'attaccante possa tornare decisivo. L'Arsenal resta interessato al classe 2000, individuato come possibile rinforzo offensivo, anche se già in estate l'ex River aveva rifiutato il trasferimento in Premier.

Nessun futuro al Fenerbahçe per Francesco Totti, almeno secondo quanto comunicato ufficialmente dal club turco. Nelle ultime ore, il quotidiano Sözcü aveva rilanciato l'ipotesi di un ingresso dell'ex capitano della Roma nella dirigenza sportiva della società di Istanbul. Una suggestione che aveva rapidamente attirato l'attenzione dei media locali, anche alla luce del momento complicato attraversato dal Fenerbahçe. Totti, attualmente senza incarichi ufficiali nel mondo del calcio, era stato indicato come una possibile figura di riferimento per rilanciare il progetto sportivo gialloblù. La società, però, ha deciso di intervenire direttamente per smentire la ricostruzione.

Il Lione ha ufficializzato l'ingaggio di Carlos Vicens come nuovo allenatore della prima squadra. La società francese ha spiegato di aver individuato nel tecnico spagnolo il profilo ideale per dare continuità al progetto sportivo, puntando su una filosofia di gioco, un metodo di lavoro e una particolare attenzione alla crescita dei calciatori pienamente in linea con gli obiettivi del club.

Raúl González Blanco torna in panchina. L'ex attaccante e leggenda del Real Madrid è ufficialmente il nuovo allenatore del Real Oviedo, formazione che nella passata stagione militava nella Liga e che attualmente occupa il nono posto nella seconda divisione spagnola. Per il tecnico quarantanovenne si tratta di una nuova esperienza professionale dopo il lungo percorso all'interno del settore giovanile dei Blancos, culminato con la guida del Real Madrid Castilla fino al 2025.

Il Real Madrid è pronto a mettere le mani su uno dei talenti più promettenti del calcio europeo. I blancos avrebbero infatti vinto la concorrenza di Barcellona, Paris Saint-Germain e Bayern Monaco per JJ Gabriel, sedicenne del Manchester United considerato un potenziale fuoriclasse. Dopo mesi di contatti e trattative, l'operazione sarebbe ormai vicina alla conclusione, con il giocatore pronto ad annunciare la propria destinazione nei prossimi giorni. I Red Devils hanno tentato in ogni modo di trattenerlo, arrivando persino a convocarlo in prima squadra per una partita di coppa quando aveva appena 15 anni. Una mossa che, però, non è bastata a modificare la sua decisione.

#2170. Rangers player becomes third Ibrox star to be snubbed by national team

Rangers defender Emmanuel Fernandez has become the third Ibrox star to be ditched for international duty after he was left out of the Nigeria squad.

The 25-year-old is a key man for Derek McInnes' side and made his 20th appearance of the season in Rangers' weekend win at St Mirren.

However, he was a surprise omission in manager Eric Chelle's squad for their Africa Cup of Nations qualification double-header against Tanzania this month.

Fernandez is the latest Rangers star to be excluded from their international squads after Kim Min-su was left out of South Korea's team and Bojan Miovski was snubbed from the North Macedonia squad, even though their next game is in Glasgow against Scotland.

Oscar Cortes transforms Rangers fortunes as stunning form revival earns Colombia call Premiership star bemoans 'unlucky' red card picked up against Rangers

Fernandez won his fourth cap last month when he played for the Super Eagles in a friendly against Russia in Nizhny Novgorod, which finished in a 3-3 draw.

But he's failed to make the list for their upcoming games.

However, Fernandez may yet get a reprieve after Hull City stopper Semi Ajayi suffered an injury against Arsenal at the weekend, with reports in Nigeria suggesting Fernandez would be first in line to replace him if Ajayi doesn't make it.

#2169. Blackburn Rovers, Birmingham City track Chelsea starlet after Tony Mowbray snubbed summer loan deal

Blackburn Rovers and Birmingham City have both performed positively over the first quarter of the 2026/27 campaign, but they’ll have eyes on the January transfer window as an opportunity to strengthen the ranks further. Now, TEAMtalk has claimed one player of interest to both clubs is Kendry Paez.

The report states that Chelsea are actively looking for potential loan destinations for Ecuadorian prospect Paez. Rovers and the Blues are keen, but the 19-year-old also has admirers at Celtic and Trabzonspor, and he was eyed by Belgium’s KV Mechelen in the summer.

Paez was on the radar at Ewood Park in the last transfer window as was put to Tony Mowbray as a potential signing. However, the boss snubbed the deal, and the attacking midfielder has remained with Chelsea instead.

Paez was viewed as one of South America’s most exciting talents when he officially joined Chelsea in 2025. However, loans at RC Strasbourg and River Plate have not yielded the success hoped.

Blackburn Rovers were lined up as a potential destination in Paez’s bid to settle into English football, but after Mowbray opted against bringing him in, he has only played U21s football.

Paez has made six appearances across the Premier League 2 and EFL Trophy. He provided an assist in a 2-1 defeat to Wycombe Wanderers in September and played a little over an hour in a 2-0 triumph over Reading in the same cup competitions in October.

At 19, the Chelsea starlet is still young and has plenty of time to come good in England. But, Paez has struggled to impact games the way he did in his emergence in Ecuador with Independiente del Valle, so it remains to be seen if a half-season loan to the Championship can breathe life into his career again.

This applies to Birmingham City more given that they are the current Championship leaders, but clubs have to be careful with loan deals in the winter. Recruitment staff and managers alike need to be wary of how new signings will impact a thriving squad.

Loan players come in with a desire to play, but it can be tough to find opportunities in a thriving team. If that were to happen to Paez, it wouldn’t help his development and could bring players off the pitch too.

Blackburn Rovers aren’t in quite the same position as they’re 13th, but Mowbray has harnessed some strong squad spirit. The fact the boss has already snubbed the loan signing of Paez would raise questions over a winter deal for his services too.

Time will tell what comes of Paez’s winter transfer fate as Chelsea look for potential destinations.

#2167. Struggling £26m West Ham man eyed for escape transfer to South American giants

West Ham signed Taty Castellanos for a deal worth around £26m in January as they bid to salvage their Premier League status. WHUFC came up short despite their new striker netting six goals in 18 Premier League games, but his form has not continued in the Championship.

Now, a report from Argentine news outlet El Intransigente says Castellanos could find an escape route back to his home country. River Plate are keen on signing a new striker and the Hammers man sits atop their shortlist of targets.

Despite being from Argentina, Castellanos has never played senior football in his native. He has played in Chile, Uruguay, Spain and the United States, as well as in England with West Ham.

West Ham have not had an easy time in the Championship, dropping to 9th after Saturday’s loss to Blackburn Rovers. However, with 37 goals scored in 15 matches, they are still the league’s highest-scoring team by far.

Despite this, and his pedigree at a higher level, Castellanos has not found form in front of goal. He started the competitive campaign with a Carabao Cup brace against Portsmouth and scored in the same competition against Southampton in August too.

But, the 28-year-old has found the back of the net just once in the Championship, that being in the 6-0 home thumping of Wrexham. Castellanos has chipped in with a strong tally of five assists, but a total of 3.46 xG shows how he is underperforming in terms of goalscoring (FotMob).

It has seen him drop from the WHUFC starting lineup for four of the last six league games. Now, it brings questions over his future as River Plate lurk.

As highlighted, West Ham are scoring plenty of goals. However, they’re not coming from the strikers.

Of the natural no. 9’s on the books, Joel Piroe is the top scorer on four. Pablo earns praise for his physicality and work rate but two goals in 28 career games for WHUFC shows he leaves a lot to be desired in front of goal.

By selling Castellanos, the Hammers can raise funds and free up space in the squad for a new talisman to join. They can ease the burden on Piroe while Pablo can mainly be used in the impact substitute role he tends to be better in.

It could breathe new life into Scott Parker’s attack with clear threats out wide and in midfield like Jarrod Bowen, Edvin Austbo, Manor Solomon, Divine Mukasa and Mohamadou Kante.

#2165. Kim Hellberg footage after Middlesbrough's draw v Watford is becoming a talking point

Middlesbrough dropped points and failed to score for the second time this week on Saturday at Watford, and Boro boss Kim Hellberg was seen visibly frustrated after the final whistle.

The draw at Vicarage Road saw Middlesbrough relinquish the top spot in the Championship ahead of the November international break, with Birmingham City climbing above them on goal difference.

It's not how Boro would have wanted to sign off this mini block of fixtures between international breaks, and the performance against Watford, especially, would have provided plenty of food for thought in the dugout.

Pick any two players. Send the code, and your mate enters it here to play the same pair.

Indeed, this was the second successive game without leading talisman and Championship top scorer Will Lankshear due to injury, and it's almost universally agreed that if the 21-year-old was leading the line on Saturday, they'd have cruised to victory, as Boro had 18 shots, 34 touches in Watford's box and four big chances, but couldn't score.

Middlesbrough boss Kim Hellberg has shown how passionate he is about the game in the 12 months he's spent in charge at the Riverside. Therefore, it was clear that a result like this, following such a dominant performance, was going to affect him.

After the game, the Swede was filmed staring blankly at the ground for around half a minute, just reflecting on the game, and Boro fans have reacted accordingly.

Some have made jokey comments about the state of the frontline when Lankshear is injured. One Boro fan took it upon themselves to caption the video, "What having Kyle Joseph as an attacking option does to man." Another commented, "Please god make Will be fit, please god make Will be fit…..🙏."

"He’s got Kyle Joseph depression," was also among the reactions to the video.

Football League World has asked AI to rank the top nine toughest fanbases in the EFL Championship right now from least to most

The majority of comments in response to Hellberg's post-match reaction were about how invested he is in the game, though, and Middlesbrough fans know how lucky they are to have the Swede in charge.

"You can just tell this isn’t performative. Like the fist bumps and the celebrations when we win, or the tears during the play-off debacle last season, Kim ‘gets it’, he’s immersed himself into the #Boro way. Never seen a manager so invested in us," one Boro fan tweeted.

"Carries it all, the highs and lows just like the fans. “A good leader leads the people from above them. A great leader leads the people from within them," was also among the comments, as was, "The reaction of a man who puts the weight of the world on his own shoulders #UTB."

"Very powerful, but also makes me want to give him a hug and tell him he's one of my favourite ever Boro gaffers and everything will be alright," someone commented in response to the video, showcasing the love and respect Boro fans have towards their head coach.

This is the reaction of a man spent after a disappointing result, having coached what was effectively the perfect game.

Plenty of head coaches nowadays are focused on the journey, the performance levels and good underlying numbers, and Hellberg is definitely one of those, but he also recognises how important it is to win games, too.

As one Boro fan mentioned, he carries the weight of expectation amongst Middlesbrough fans, who are desperate to return to the Premier League after a decade away, and you can imagine he takes not winning as seriously as some of those in the stands do, too.

It's reactions like this that are why many are touting Hellberg to become a top-flight manager in the near future, and Boro supporters hope it'll be with them.

However, it's hard to ignore the fact that, while Lankshear sits on the sidelines, Middlesbrough struggles to score goals and win games.

Boro's wasteful nature in front of goal dominated the narrative surrounding them in the final few months of last season as they slipped out of the running for automatic promotion and into the play-offs, and they could have been out of sight in the first half of their play-off semi-final first leg against Southampton if not for poor finishing, too.

Lankshear has papered over those cracks, as he's been brilliant at finishing the plenty of chances Middlesbrough create in a game, but the alarm bells are starting to ring without him.

Do you think Middlesbrough can turn their finishing troubles around without Will Lankshear in the side? Let us know in the comments.

Hopefully, the 21-year-old will be back after the international break and won't suffer any more injury setbacks, as that could be the difference between promotion and some grim de ja vu.

It's something Hellberg will be mulling over for the next two weeks, and it seemingly started immediately after the final whistle at Watford.

#2164. National League clubs interested as 31-year-old leaves Millwall by mutual consent

Millwall have had experienced goalkeeper Joel Coleman on the books as cover since the summer of 2025. He is yet to make an appearance for the Lions and was left out of their 25-man EFL squad for the ongoing campaign.

Now, writing on X, reporter Pete O’Rourke states that Coleman and the club have come to an agreement over the mutual termination of his contract. It frees the 31-year-old to depart and find a new team outside of the transfer window, and there are National League sides keen on the shot-stopper.

Coleman has played the majority of football across his career in League One, but he has appeared in League Two and the Championship too. He was in three matchday squads for Alex Neil‘s side this season prior to his omission.

Including Coleman, Millwall have had five goalkeepers on their books this season. Even with Lukas Jensen and Filip Marschall cast to the sidelines through injury, the former Bolton Wanderers and Huddersfield Town man has been third choice behind Max Crocombe and George Evans for the most part.

Injuries can bring opportunities for the backup ‘keepers, but the Lions’ depth in this position means it would take a serious crisis, dismal form from several players or both for Coleman to come into contention for minutes in Neil’s side.

It means the Championship club can afford to let him go mid-season without signing a replacement. Coleman joined on a free transfer as well, so they’re not taking a big loss in terminating his contract either.

It remains to be seen just where he winds up after his contract end, but this move should benefit Coleman.

Coleman has spent much of his career as a backup goalkeeper, but his minutes have dried up in recent times especially. He never played for Millwall and he didn’t appear in his final season at Bolton Wanderers in 2024/25 either.

Having kept training, the Bolton-born shot-stopper will hope he can move to a National League club and quickly become a hit. He’ll have plenty of experience and pedigree to offer so could become an influential asset on and off the pitch.

Coleman’s best season came as he broke through at Oldham Athletic, keeping 12 clean sheets and conceding 35 goals in 32 League One appearances across the 2015/16 season. He will hope to tap back into that form in the bid to become a starting shot-stopper again.

#2163. West Ham face Taty Castellanos, River Plate transfer exit threat

As West Ham continue to fall short of rekindling their fortunes despite Scott Parker's recent appointment, the possibility of Taty Castellanos departing the London Stadium in the coming months has now emerged in a development that will spark mixed reactions from Hammers supporters.

West Ham's return to the Championship following 14 successive seasons of solid Premier League standing is not going to plan right now, with the East Londoners appearing increasingly unlikely to seal a previously-expected top-flight return at the very first time of asking as champions.

A mixed run of form featuring consecutive home defeats at home to a pair of promotion rivals in Southampton and West Bromwich Albion ultimately ended Nuno Espirito Santo's unpopular tenure in charge, and the Portuguese boss has been swiftly succeeded by former Hammers midfielder Parker.

The ex-Bournemouth and Fulham boss, having already achieved three promotions from the second-tier with as many clubs after most recently guiding Burnley back to the promised land in 2025 before his dismissal towards the end of the previous campaign, has encountered a challenging return to the club thus far, though, taking just one point from his opening two matches.

Last Wednesday's thrilling 3-3 comeback draw at Cardiff City after overturning a three-goal defecit at half-time was followed up by a disappointing 2-1 defeat away to Blackburn Rovers over the weekend, and with the Hammers yet to enjoy the anticipated upturn under Parker, they now face the threat of losing one of their big-name performers once the January window rolls around.

Castellanos has endured a particularly difficult and underwhelming introduction to the Championship, which the Argentine forward was expected to light up after his mid-season arrival back in January preceded encouraging goalscoring form in spite of his side's ultimate failure to dodge the drop.

Six goals from 18 Premier League appearances means that the current return of just one strike across 14 second-tier fixtures - nine of which have been starts - is not what fans were anticipating, and they had presented vocal criticism towards the divisive 28-year-old for a number of weeks before Parker hauled the attacker off at the interval against the Bluebirds and then dropped his name out of the starting line-up at Ewood Park.

It has now emerged that Castellanos could leave East London as early as January, with a fresh report from Argentine-based outlet El Intransigente claiming strong long-term interest from River Plate.

As per the update, River Plate have held interest in the two-cap Argentine international's services in recent windows and he represents a "top target" in the eyes of manager Leonardo Ponzio.

Blackburn Rovers captain Todd Cantwell has been among those reacting to the club's dig towards Scott Parker's West Ham after Saturday's 2-1 victory

It's said that the Buenos Aries-based giants feel strongly about Castellano's profile and international experience, with the striker having risen to prominence outside of his native Argentina in Uruguay with Montevideo City Torque before enjoying productive spells at New York City, Girona and Lazio ahead of joining West Ham earlier this year for a reported £26 million figure on a four-and-a-half year contract.

West Ham supporters have grown increasingly critical of Castellanos and his long-term future under Parker remains unclear at this moment in time, although a fresh hurdle could prevent River Plate from sealing the deal.

River Plate have been presented with a significant obstacle to overcome if they intend on returning Castellanos to his homeland, as it's a move which wouldn't come cheaply for the 38-time Primera Division champions.

Indeed, the report has added that the salary Castellanos earns at the London Stadium is the main challenge for River Plate right now, leaving the club to weigh up alternative options such as acquiring the sought-after frontman on loan or even purchasing a percentage of his rights.

Both of those options, of course, would require the prior approval of West Ham, whose own stance on potentially parting ways with the ex-Lazio striker just a year after his arrival with a club likely unable to recoup the figure of purchase is also not yet known.

It's noted that Castellanos' lengthy contract with the Championship promotion hopefuls puts Parker's side in a comfortable negotiating decision, and hold all the cards as River Plate will not be able to wrap up any deal without their support.

Interestingly, however, the update also makes reference to previous interest elsewhere, reminding that Castellanos had reportedly wished to leave West Ham in the summer amid loan interest from Seville-based rivals Real Betis and Sevilla that never materialised.

The London Stadium faithful remain very much divided on Castellanos at the moment, with some making their minds up on the out-of-form forward and others willing to persist with further patience.

Prior claims of a January exit if the price is met at the end of the previous month saw some West Ham fans refuse to hold back on their feelings about Castellanos' future, and this one fan believes that his hapless form in the Championship would make for no great loss if he was to depart.

Another would be happy to see the Hammers striker leave at any cost, even if the club themselves surely won't take that stance.

Castellanos has left some frustrated for reasons beyond his inability to find the back of the net on a regular basis, too, and having formerly scored at a relatively-prolific rate leading the line for a side relegated from the Premier League, his work-rate and application have been called into question.

His display in the aforementioned draw at Cardiff drew particular criticism, and West Ham were a much more fluid, functional and threatening outfit once the Argentine was withdrawn for Joel Piroe at the break.

Now, with the Suriname international scoring in successive encounters, it's unclear how Castellanos will force his way back into starting contention ahead of a positional rival with a historically-goal-laden hit-rate at this level.

West Ham fans, how do you feel about Taty Castellanos' future? Would you hold onto the striker, or is it time to cash in? What should his valuation be? Let us know your thoughts in the comments below!

But there are other fans who continue to stand by Castellanos, and considering how he fared at the highest level last time out, it's no great surprise that certain supporters still believe he can come good in the second-tier.

Either way, there are plenty of West Ham fans who, despite the criticism of the majority, do feel that the divisive striker would thrive in a system that emphasises chance creation under a manager willing to trust in his services and get the best out of an undeniably-talented striker with pedigree in more prestigious and higher-quality divisions.

This punter, meanwhile, reckons that cashing in on Castellanos would be a "stupid decision", but only time will tell to see what action West Ham decide to take and whether a deal can successfully be thrashed out with River Plate.

#2162. Manchester United player ratings: United failed to win despite advantage

Shumail Sajid writes about Manchester United for Read Man Utd and Crystal Palace for Read Crystal Palace, with occasional pieces on other Premier League clubs. He has also written for FanSided's Red Devils Dispatch, Yardbarker, Anfield Central and The Sports Side.

Manchester United and Tottenham Hotspur returned from the international break with plenty to prove after a disastrous start to the season.

United arrived with just one win, while Tottenham came to Old Trafford without a win.

Both teams had half-chances in the first half but little to show for them. Tottenham had the better chances with chances for Dominic Solanke, Connor Gallagher and Kudus, but none could take advantage of them.

Manchester United came close in the first half to score after Bryan Mbeumo raced towards the goal on a counter-attack to find Amad. He had a difficult shot, and eventually the shot was too weak to cause any damage. The first-half display was met by boos from the Old Trafford crowd at half-time and during the first half as well.

Solanke had another chance at the start of the second half but put it over the top, amid a wrestle with Lisandro Martinez in the six-yard box. It was United, however, who started the second half with promise.

Substitutions by Michael Carrick around the hour mark made the difference as United showed intent before Mbeumo got the goal off Cunha’s low cross. Luke Shaw was at the end of a scuffle with Bentancur, who was shown a red card for headbutting Shaw.

United failed to capitalize on their one-man advantage and seemed to sit on their 1-0 lead, but Solanke punished them with a header to level the game in injury time. The draw left United with just 6 points off their first six games. The result leaves United with their worst start in 40 years.

Lenne Sammens-6          Quiet afternoon for United no.1 till the goal, where he saw the ball go into the back of the net.

Leny Yoro-6       Defensively was fine but offered little upfront. Played out of position, and he showed good defensive awareness on the right back till his substitution.

Harry Maguire-5.5            Was decent in the first half but wasn’t much of an answer to Tottenham going forward in the second half

Lisandro Martinez-5      Took his eyes off the ball, resulting in conceding a late goal off Solanke.

Luke Shaw-6      Went up high on the pitch and remained a threat with his overlapping threat. Was involved in the scuffle that got Bentancur a red card for headbutting.

Kobbie Mainoo-6.5        Brilliant defensive tackle to deny Tottenham in the 68th minute. Remained composed in tight spaces against a physical Tottenham midfield.

Youri Tielemans-6          A decent performance and was solid throughout the minutes he was on the field

Bruno Fernandes-5        Another quiet and disappointing evening for the United captain after he failed to impress on a day he needed to get a result for United.

Amad-6              A positive return from injury, showed plenty of promise on the wing but failed to get past a resolute Tottenham side

Matheus Cunha-7           One of the liveliest options for United going forward. Provided an assist to Mbeumo to put United up front

Bryan Mbeumo-7.5        A brilliant finish off Matheus Cunha’s low ball to put United in front. Was dangerous going forward and denied a second on a counter by Kinsky off a brilliant long ball by Baleba.

Carlos Baleba (58’)-6.5  A decent debut overall for the Cameroon international. He put in a couple of dangerous balls up the field, including a clear ball to Mbeumo on a counter, who was denied by Kinsky.

Diogo Dalot (58’)-6      Provided a decent attacking threat when brought on around the hour mark.

Joshua Zirkzee (58’)-6   Showed plenty of promise and was a threat after he was brought in around the hour mark.

#2161. Birmingham City, Celtic & Blackburn eye Chelsea man who could be 'better than Messi'

Blackburn Rovers and Birmingham City are both keen on a deal to sign Kendry Paez from Chelsea.

According to Teamtalk, the two Championship sides are both keen on the 19-year-old ahead of the January transfer window.

The teenager, who has been tipped to potentially be 'better than Messi' by ex-Ecuador international Carlos Tenorio, is currently a part of the first team squad at Stamford Bridge after returning early from a loan spell with River Plate.

However, he is not a part of Xabi Alonso’s plans at the moment, and has yet to play a single minute for the Spaniard this season.

The attacking-midfielder could be available in the new year, with the Premier League side seeking a loan move in order for Paez to gain valuable experience.

Chelsea signed the Ecuadorian in 2025 in a deal worth £17.3 million, but he has yet to make his debut for the club.

It’s believed that both Birmingham and Blackburn are keen on a deal to sign Paez when the winter market opens in January.

However, the pair will face competition from Scottish giants Celtic, who are also interested in signing the 19-year-old.

The playmaker previously spent time on loan with Strasbourg, with the French club owned by the same group as the Blues.

Jude and Jobe Bellingham have been named in Thomas Tuchel's senior Three Lions squad together for the first time

But he was only capable of making 15 appearances in Ligue 1, including four starts, scoring on just one occasion before going to River Plate January 2026.

It’s understood that Chelsea have been encouraged by his performances at underage level, and are now seeking a loan deal again in the upcoming January market.

Blackburn and Birmingham are both keen on a deal, but there are clubs from across Europe keeping tabs on the player’s progress, including Trabzonspor and KV Mechelen.

The Belgian side reportedly made an approach last summer, and are aiming to be at the front of the queue again in the new year.

Birmingham moved into top spot in the Championship table last weekend ahead of the November international break, with a 2-1 win against Swansea City lifting them ahead of Middlesbrough in second.

Meanwhile, Blackburn Rovers moved up to 13th with a 2-1 win of their own at home to West Ham, with eight points separating the two clubs chasing Paez’s signature.

Chris Davies’ side will be targeting promotion back to the Premier League in the second half of the campaign, while the Lancashire outfit will be aiming to break into the top eight.

Birmingham will return to action against Stoke City on 21 November, while Rovers will travel to face bitter rivals Burnley the following afternoon.

Paez wasn’t ready for top flight football in France last year, and his underwhelming performances reflected that.

But he’s now had time with Chelsea’s underage team to develop his game and better prepare for senior football, as he is still only 19 and moving from South America to Europe isn’t an easy transition.

Would Kendry Paez be a good signing for Blackburn or Birmingham? Comment your opinion below

There are no guarantees that his improvement is real because the step up from youth football to senior minutes is tough, but this move could be a gamble worth taking.

If Paez can make that step up, then he could bring the kind of attacking flair and creativity in the second half of the season that can bolster a promotion push for either Birmingham or Blackburn.
